```json
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Dyrnwch Gawr adalah raksasa (cawr) dalam tradisi Welsh yang dikenal sebagai pemilik kuali ajaib, salah satu dari Tiga Belas Pusaka Pulau Britania; kuali itu tidak mau merebus daging untuk pengecut.",
  "en": "Dyrnwch Gawr is a giant (cawr) of Welsh tradition known as the owner of a magic cauldron, one of the Thirteen Treasures of the Island of Britain; the cauldron will not boil meat for a coward.",

 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Dyrnwch Gawr, juga dieja Dyrnhwch Gawr, adalah raksasa Welsh (cawr Cymreig) yang dikaitkan dengan Hen Ogledd dan dengan Gwent. Naskah-naskah daftar pusaka juga memuat ejaan Dyrnog dan Tyrnog. Selain dalam daftar pusaka, namanya muncul dalam daftar raksasa Cymru yang disusun Siôn Dafydd Rhys pada akhir abad ke-16, dengan keterangan bahwa ia berada di negeri Ewias.",
   "en": "Dyrnwch Gawr, also spelled Dyrnhwch Gawr, is a Welsh giant (cawr Cymreig) associated with the Hen Ogledd and with Gwent. The manuscripts of the treasure list also give the spellings Dyrnog and Tyrnog. Besides the treasure list, his name appears in the list of the giants of Cymru compiled by Siôn Dafydd Rhys at the end of the 16th century, which places him in the land of Ewias.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c01",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c07",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c08",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c13"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Ia terutama dikenal sebagai pemilik kuali istimewa dalam daftar Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain (Tiga Belas Pusaka Pulau Britania). Kuali itu membedakan pemberani dari pengecut: daging untuk orang pengecut tidak akan pernah mendidih di dalamnya, sedangkan daging untuk orang pemberani cepat matang. Pusaka-pusaka itu digambarkan sebagai benda ajaib tradisi Welsh yang disebut dalam naskah abad ke-15 dan ke-16, dan kebanyakan dikaitkan dengan 'Utara'.",
   "en": "He is known chiefly as the owner of a special cauldron in the list Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain (the Thirteen Treasures of the Island of Britain). The cauldron tells the brave from the cowardly: meat for a coward will never boil in it, while meat for a brave man cooks quickly. The treasures are described as magical items of Welsh tradition mentioned in 15th- and 16th-century manuscripts, most of them linked with 'The North'.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c02",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c03",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c11",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c14",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c15",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c16"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Kisah kuali ini hampir pasti bersumber dari rujukan pada 'kuali Diwrnach Wyddel' dalam kisah Culhwch ac Olwen, dan karena nama Dyrnwch dan Diwrnach sangat mirip, nama pertama kemungkinan bentuk yang lebih muda dari yang kedua. Sifat serupa dikenakan pada kuali Pen Annwn dalam puisi 'Preiddiau Annwn' di Llyfr Taliesin, yang tidak merebus makanan pengecut. Walaupun pusaka-pusaka itu dikaitkan dengan Hen Ogledd, Dyrnwch tidak disebut dalam silsilah seperti Bonedd Gwŷr y Gogledd.",
   "en": "The story of the cauldron is almost certainly based on a reference to 'the cauldron of Diwrnach Wyddel' in the tale Culhwch ac Olwen, and because the names Dyrnwch and Diwrnach are so alike, the former is probably a later form of the latter. A similar virtue is given to the cauldron of Pen Annwn in the poem 'Preiddiau Annwn' in Llyfr Taliesin, which does not boil a coward's food. Although the treasures are linked with the Hen Ogledd, Dyrnwch is not mentioned in genealogies such as Bonedd Gwŷr y Gogledd.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c04",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c05",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c06",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c09",
    "dyrnwch-gawr-c12"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Culhwch ac Olwen juga memuat tokoh lain bernama Wrnach Gawr, pemilik sebilah pedang istimewa. Mungkin ada hubungan antara Wrnach Gawr dan Diwrnach Wyddel, dan Dyrnwch Gawr mungkin perkembangan lebih kemudian dari tokoh-tokoh itu, tetapi hal ini tidak dapat dibuktikan secara pasti.",
   "en": "Culhwch ac Olwen also contains another character called Wrnach Gawr, who owns a special sword. There may be a link between Wrnach Gawr and Diwrnach Wyddel, and Dyrnwch Gawr may be a later development based on those characters, but this cannot be proven conclusively.",

 "claims": [
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c01",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Cawr Cymreig a gysylltir â'r Hen Ogledd ac â Gwent yw Dyrnwch Gawr (amrywiad, Dyrnhwch Gawr).",
   "locator": "Paragraf pembuka",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dyrnwch Gawr (varian: Dyrnhwch Gawr) adalah raksasa Welsh (cawr Cymreig) yang dikaitkan dengan Hen Ogledd dan Gwent.",
    "en": "Dyrnwch Gawr (variant: Dyrnhwch Gawr) is a Welsh giant (cawr Cymreig) associated with the Hen Ogledd and with Gwent."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c02",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Enwir Dyrnwch Gawr yn y rhestr Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain fel perchennog pair arbennig",
   "locator": "Paragraf 2",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dyrnwch Gawr disebut dalam daftar Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain sebagai pemilik (perchennog) kuali (pair) istimewa.",
    "en": "Dyrnwch Gawr is named in the list Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain as the owner (perchennog) of a special cauldron (pair)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c03",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "pe rhoid ynddo gig i ŵr llwfr i ferwi, ni ferwai fyth; o rhoid iddo gig i ŵr dewr, berwi a wnâi yn ebrwydd",
   "locator": "Kutipan Pair Dyrnwch Gawr",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Jika daging (cig) untuk orang pengecut (llwfr) dimasukkan untuk direbus, daging itu tidak akan pernah mendidih (ni ferwai fyth); jika daging untuk orang pemberani (dewr), ia cepat mendidih (berwi).",
    "en": "If meat (cig) for a coward (llwfr) were put in to boil, it would never boil (ni ferwai fyth); if meat for a brave man (dewr), it would boil (berwi) quickly."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c04",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Priodolir rhinwedd cyffelyb i'r Pair Pen Annwn y cyfeirir ato yn y gerdd 'Preiddiau Annwn' yn Llyfr Taliesin, sydd ddim yn berwi bwyd llwfrgi.",
   "locator": "Paragraf 3",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Sifat serupa dikenakan pada Pair Pen Annwn yang disebut dalam puisi 'Preiddiau Annwn' di Llyfr Taliesin, yang tidak merebus (berwi) makanan pengecut (llwfrgi).",
    "en": "A similar virtue is attributed to the Pair Pen Annwn mentioned in the poem 'Preiddiau Annwn' in Llyfr Taliesin, which does not boil (berwi) a coward's (llwfrgi) food."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c05",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Mae hi bron yn sicr fod y stori am y pair yn seiliedig ar gyfeiriad yn y chwedl Culhwch ac Olwen am \"bair Diwrnach Wyddel\".",
   "locator": "Paragraf 4",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Hampir pasti kisah tentang kuali (pair) itu didasarkan pada rujukan dalam kisah Culhwch ac Olwen tentang 'bair Diwrnach Wyddel' (kuali Diwrnach Wyddel).",
    "en": "It is almost certain that the story of the cauldron (pair) is based on a reference in the tale Culhwch ac Olwen to 'bair Diwrnach Wyddel' (the cauldron of Diwrnach Wyddel)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c06",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Cysylltir y Tri Thlws ar Ddeg â'r Hen Ogledd, ond ni cheir cyfeiriad at Ddyrnwch yn yr achau (e.e. Bonedd Gwŷr y Gogledd).",
   "locator": "Paragraf 5",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Tri Thlws ar Ddeg dikaitkan dengan Hen Ogledd, tetapi Dyrnwch (Ddyrnwch) tidak disebut dalam silsilah (achau), misalnya Bonedd Gwŷr y Gogledd.",
    "en": "The Tri Thlws ar Ddeg are associated with the Hen Ogledd, but Dyrnwch (Ddyrnwch) is not mentioned in the genealogies (achau), e.g. Bonedd Gwŷr y Gogledd."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c07",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "ceir enw Dyrn(h)wch gan Siôn Dafydd Rhys ar ddiwedd y 16g yn ei restr o gewri Cymru",
   "locator": "Paragraf 5",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Nama Dyrn(h)wch tercantum dalam daftar (rhestr) raksasa Cymru (gewri Cymru) karya Siôn Dafydd Rhys pada akhir abad ke-16 (16g).",
    "en": "The name Dyrn(h)wch is given by Siôn Dafydd Rhys at the end of the 16th century (16g) in his list (rhestr) of the giants of Cymru (gewri Cymru)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c08",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Dyrnhwch Gawr yng ngwlad Ewias.",
   "locator": "Kutipan dari daftar Siôn Dafydd Rhys",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dyrnhwch Gawr di negeri (gwlad) Ewias.",
    "en": "Dyrnhwch Gawr in the land (gwlad) of Ewias."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c09",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Am fod yr enwau Dyrnwch a Diwrnach mor debyg i'w gilydd mae'n debygol fod y cyntaf yn ffurf ddiweddar ar yr ail",
   "locator": "Paragraf 5",
   "context": "etymology",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Karena nama (enwau) Dyrnwch dan Diwrnach sangat mirip, kemungkinan yang pertama adalah bentuk lebih muda (ffurf ddiweddar) dari yang kedua.",
    "en": "Because the names (enwau) Dyrnwch and Diwrnach are so alike, the former is probably a later form (ffurf ddiweddar) of the latter."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c10",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s1",
   "quote": "Efallai fod cysylltiad rhwng Wrnach Gawr a Diwrnach Wyddel, er bod yr hanesion amdanynt yn wahanol, a bod Dyrnwch Gawr yn ddatblygiad diweddarach seiliedig ar y cymeriadau hynny, ond nid oes modd profi hynny'n derfynol.",
   "locator": "Paragraf 6",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Mungkin ada hubungan (cysylltiad) antara Wrnach Gawr dan Diwrnach Wyddel, dan Dyrnwch Gawr mungkin perkembangan lebih kemudian (datblygiad diweddarach) dari tokoh-tokoh itu, tetapi tidak dapat dibuktikan secara pasti.",
    "en": "There may be a link (cysylltiad) between Wrnach Gawr and Diwrnach Wyddel, and Dyrnwch Gawr may be a later development (datblygiad diweddarach) of those characters, but this cannot be proven conclusively."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c11",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s2",
   "quote": "The cauldron (pair) of Dyrnwch the Giant is said to discriminate between cowards and brave men: whereas it would not boil meat for a coward, it would boil quickly if that meat belonged to a brave man.",
   "locator": "Cauldron of Dyrnwch the Giant",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Kuali (pair) Dyrnwch sang Raksasa dikatakan membedakan pengecut dari pemberani: ia tidak merebus daging untuk pengecut, tetapi cepat merebus daging milik orang pemberani.",
    "en": "The cauldron (pair) of Dyrnwch the Giant is said to discriminate between cowards and brave men: it would not boil meat for a coward but would boil quickly meat belonging to a brave man."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c12",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s2",
   "quote": "Although Dyrnwch is not himself described as an Irishman, it is probable that his name goes back to Diwrnach.",
   "locator": "Cauldron of Dyrnwch the Giant",
   "context": "etymology",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Walaupun Dyrnwch sendiri tidak disebut orang Irlandia (Irishman), kemungkinan besar namanya berasal dari Diwrnach.",
    "en": "Although Dyrnwch is not himself described as an Irishman, his name probably goes back to Diwrnach."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c13",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s2",
   "quote": "The extant manuscripts of Tri Thlws ar Ddeg also present such variant spellings as Dyrnog and Tyrnog, without the Irish-sounding ending",
   "locator": "Cauldron of Dyrnwch the Giant",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Naskah-naskah Tri Thlws ar Ddeg yang masih ada juga memuat ejaan varian Dyrnog dan Tyrnog, tanpa akhiran yang berbunyi Irlandia.",
    "en": "The extant manuscripts of Tri Thlws ar Ddeg also give the variant spellings Dyrnog and Tyrnog, without the Irish-sounding ending."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c14",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s3",
   "quote": "Pair Dyrnwch Gawr: The Cauldron of Dyrnwch the Giant",
   "locator": "Daftar pusaka, nomor 7",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Pair Dyrnwch Gawr berarti Kuali Dyrnwch sang Raksasa (The Cauldron of Dyrnwch the Giant).",
    "en": "Pair Dyrnwch Gawr means The Cauldron of Dyrnwch the Giant."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c15",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s3",
   "quote": "These treasures are ancient magical items of Welsh tradition that are mentioned in 15th and 16th Century manuscripts. Most of the treasures are from and in ‘The North’ of the Island of Britain.",
   "locator": "Pengantar",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Pusaka-pusaka (treasures) ini adalah benda ajaib kuno tradisi Welsh yang disebut dalam naskah abad ke-15 dan ke-16; kebanyakan berasal dari 'The North' (Utara) Pulau Britania.",
    "en": "These treasures are ancient magical items of Welsh tradition mentioned in 15th and 16th century manuscripts; most of them are from 'The North' of the Island of Britain."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "dyrnwch-gawr-c16",
   "source_id": "dyrnwch-gawr-s2",
   "quote": "The Thirteen Treasures of the Island of Britain (Welsh: Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain) are a series of items in late-medieval Welsh tradition.",
   "locator": "Lead",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Tiga Belas Pusaka Pulau Britania (Thirteen Treasures of the Island of Britain; Welsh: Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain) adalah serangkaian benda dalam tradisi Welsh akhir Abad Pertengahan.",
    "en": "The Thirteen Treasures of the Island of Britain (Welsh: Tri Thlws ar Ddeg Ynys Prydain) are a series of items in late-medieval Welsh tradition."
   }

 ],
```

```json
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Eryr Gwern Abwy, Elang Gwern Abwy, adalah salah satu Hewan Tertua dalam mitologi Wales yang muncul dalam kisah Culhwch ac Olwen dan menuntun para utusan Arthur kepada Salmon dari Llyn Llyw.",
  "en": "The Eagle of Gwern Abwy (Eryr Gwern Abwy) is one of the Oldest Animals of the mythology of Wales who appears in the tale of Culhwch ac Olwen and guides Arthur's envoys to the Salmon of Llyn Llyw.",

 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Eryr Gwern Abwy adalah salah satu Hewan Tertua (Anifeiliaid Hynaf) dalam mitologi Wales dan disebut dalam kisah Culhwch ac Olwen. Dalam Trioedd ia termasuk Tri Hynaif Byd bersama Tylluan Cwm Cowlyd dan Mwyalchen Gelli Gadarn. Lokasi Gwern Abwy sendiri tidak diketahui. Elang adalah burung penting dalam mitologi bangsa Kelt (Celtiaid) dan dikaitkan dengan Matahari.",
   "en": "Eryr Gwern Abwy is one of the Oldest Animals (Anifeiliaid Hynaf) in the mythology of Wales and is mentioned in the tale Culhwch ac Olwen. In the Trioedd he is among the Tri Hynaif Byd alongside Tylluan Cwm Cowlyd and Mwyalchen Gelli Gadarn. The location of Gwern Abwy itself is unknown. The eagle (eryr) is an important bird in the mythology of the Celtiaid and is associated with the Sun.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "eryr-gwern-abwy-c01",
    "eryr-gwern-abwy-c03",
    "eryr-gwern-abwy-c04",
    "eryr-gwern-abwy-c05"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Dalam terjemahan kisah itu, burung hantu menuntun para utusan ke Elang Gwern Abwy, yang disebut sebagai makhluk paling tua sekaligus paling jauh mengembara. Elang itu bercerita tentang sebuah batu yang dahulu ia patuk bintang-bintangnya dari puncak dan kini tingginya tak sampai sejengkal. Para utusan menanyakan Mabon, putra Modron.",
   "en": "In the translation of the tale, the owl leads the envoys to the Eagle of Gwern Abwy, described as the most ancient creature and the greatest traveller. The eagle speaks of a rock from whose summit he once pecked at the stars, now no more than a span tall. The envoys ask after Mabon the son of Modron.",
   "claim_ids": [

    "eryr-gwern-abwy-c11"

  {
   "id": "Elang itu tidak tahu di mana Mabon berada, kecuali sekali ketika ia mencari makan sampai ke Llyn Llyw dan mencengkeram seekor salmon yang menyeretnya ke kedalaman. Ia lalu menuntun para utusan kepada salmon itu, Eog Llyn Llyw, yang diduga tahu lebih banyak.",
   "en": "The eagle does not know where Mabon is, except that once, searching for food as far as Llyn Llyw, he struck a salmon that dragged him into the deep. He then guides the envoys to that salmon, Eog Llyn Llyw, who may know more.",

 "claims": [
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c01",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s1",
   "quote": "Un o'r Anifeiliaid Hynaf yn chwedloniaeth Cymru yw Eryr Gwern Abwy. Cyfeirir at yr eryr mytholegol hwn yn chwedl Culhwch ac Olwen.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Eryr Gwern Abwy (elang Gwern Abwy) adalah salah satu Hewan Tertua (Anifeiliaid Hynaf) dalam mitologi Wales (Cymru) dan disebut dalam kisah Culhwch ac Olwen.",
    "en": "Eryr Gwern Abwy (the Eagle of Gwern Abwy) is one of the Oldest Animals (Anifeiliaid Hynaf) in the mythology of Wales (Cymru) and is mentioned in the tale Culhwch ac Olwen."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c02",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s1",
   "quote": "Cyfarwydda'r dylluan hwy at Eryr Gwern Abwy. Ni ŵyr yr eryr, ond mae'n eu tywys at Eog Llyn Llyw",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Burung hantu (tylluan) mengarahkan para utusan kepada Eryr Gwern Abwy; elang itu tidak tahu, tetapi menuntun mereka kepada Eog Llyn Llyw (Salmon dari Llyn Llyw).",
    "en": "The owl (tylluan) directs the envoys to Eryr Gwern Abwy; the eagle does not know, but guides them to Eog Llyn Llyw (the Salmon of Llyn Llyw)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c03",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s1",
   "quote": "Yn y Trioedd, enwir Tri Hynaif Byd fel Tylluan Cwm Cowlyd, Eryr Gwern Abwy a Mwyalchen Gelli Gadarn.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dalam Trioedd (Triad), Tri Hynaif Byd (Tiga Tertua di Dunia) disebut sebagai Tylluan Cwm Cowlyd, Eryr Gwern Abwy, dan Mwyalchen Gelli Gadarn.",
    "en": "In the Trioedd (Triads), the Tri Hynaif Byd (Three Oldest of the World) are named as Tylluan Cwm Cowlyd, Eryr Gwern Abwy and Mwyalchen Gelli Gadarn."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c04",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s1",
   "quote": "Mae'r eryr yn aderyn pwysig ym mytholeg y Celtiaid. Fe'i cysylltir â'r Haul.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Elang (eryr) adalah burung penting dalam mitologi bangsa Kelt (y Celtiaid) dan dikaitkan dengan Matahari (yr Haul).",
    "en": "The eagle (eryr) is an important bird in Celtic mythology (y Celtiaid) and is associated with the Sun (yr Haul)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c05",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s1",
   "quote": "Mae lleoliad Gwern Abwy yn anhysbys.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Lokasi Gwern Abwy tidak diketahui (anhysbys).",
    "en": "The location of Gwern Abwy is unknown (anhysbys)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c06",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s2",
   "quote": "the place where is the oldest animal in this world, and the one that has travelled most, the Eagle of Gwern Abwy.",
   "locator": "Kilhwch and Olwen",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Burung hantu menuntun para utusan ke tempat hewan tertua di dunia ini, yang paling banyak mengembara, Elang Gwern Abwy.",
    "en": "The owl guides the envoys to the oldest animal in this world, and the one that has travelled most, the Eagle of Gwern Abwy."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c07",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s2",
   "quote": "when I first came hither there was a rock here, from the top of which I pecked at the stars every evening; and now it is not so much as a span high.",
   "locator": "Kilhwch and Olwen",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Elang berkata saat pertama datang ada batu yang puncaknya ia patuk bintang-bintang setiap malam, dan kini batu itu tingginya tak sampai sejengkal.",
    "en": "The Eagle says that when he first came there was a rock from whose top he pecked at the stars every evening, and now it is not so much as a span high."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c08",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s2",
   "quote": "I have never heard of the man for whom you inquire, except once when I went in search of food as far as Llyn Llyw.",
   "locator": "Kilhwch and Olwen",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Elang berkata ia tidak pernah mendengar tentang orang yang dicari, kecuali sekali ketika mencari makan sampai ke Llyn Llyw.",
    "en": "The Eagle says he has never heard of the man they seek, except once when he went in search of food as far as Llyn Llyw."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c09",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s2",
   "quote": "I struck my talons into a salmon, thinking he would serve me as food for a long time. But he drew me into the deep, and I was scarcely able to escape from him.",
   "locator": "Kilhwch and Olwen",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Di Llyn Llyw elang mencengkeramkan cakarnya ke seekor salmon, tetapi salmon itu menyeretnya ke kedalaman dan ia nyaris tak bisa lolos.",
    "en": "At Llyn Llyw the Eagle struck his talons into a salmon, but the salmon drew him into the deep and he could scarcely escape."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c10",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s2",
   "quote": "Unless he know something of him whom you seek, I cannot tell who may. However, I will guide you to the place where he is.",
   "locator": "Kilhwch and Olwen",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Elang berkata hanya sang salmon yang mungkin tahu, dan ia sanggup menuntun para utusan ke tempat salmon itu berada.",
    "en": "The Eagle says only the salmon may know, and offers to guide the envoys to where he is."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c11",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s2",
   "quote": "we have come to thee an embassy from Arthur, to ask thee if thou knowest aught of Mabon the son of Modron",
   "locator": "Kilhwch and Olwen",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Para utusan Arthur bertanya kepada Elang Gwern Abwy apakah ia tahu sesuatu tentang Mabon putra Modron.",
    "en": "Arthur's envoys ask the Eagle of Gwern Abwy whether he knows anything of Mabon the son of Modron."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-c12",
   "source_id": "eryr-gwern-abwy-s2",
   "quote": "Salmon of Llyn Llyw, I have come to thee with an embassy from Arthur",
   "locator": "Kilhwch and Olwen",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Elang menyapa Salmon dari Llyn Llyw sebagai utusan dari Arthur.",
    "en": "The Eagle addresses the Salmon of Llyn Llyw as an embassy from Arthur."
   }
  }
 ],

   "searched": "Tidak ada kutipan yang menyebut tahun pencatatan kisah Culhwch ac Olwen."
  }
 ]
}
```

```json
 "traits": [],
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Fat Lips (Fatlips) adalah roh legendaris yang dikaitkan dengan Dryburgh Abbey di Berwickshire, Skotlandia. Ia digambarkan sebagai lelaki kecil bersepatu besi yang disebut-sebut merapikan ruang bawah tanah tempat tinggal seorang perempuan pengembara.",
  "en": "Fat Lips (Fatlips) is a legendary spirit linked with Dryburgh Abbey in Berwickshire, Scotland. He is described as a little man in iron shoes who was said to keep tidy the vault where a female wanderer lived.",
  "claim_ids": [
   "fat-lips-c01",

  ]

 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Fat Lips adalah nama roh legendaris yang menghuni Dryburgh Abbey di Berwickshire, Skotlandia. Kisahnya terikat pada seorang perempuan tunawisma yang tinggal di ruang bawah tanah di bawah reruntuhan biara itu beberapa waktu setelah pemberontakan Jacobite 1745.",
   "en": "Fat Lips is the name of a legendary spirit said to dwell in Dryburgh Abbey in Berwickshire, Scotland. His story is bound to a homeless woman who lived in a vault beneath the abbey ruins some time after the 1745 Jacobite rising.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "fat-lips-c01",
    "fat-lips-c02",
    "fat-lips-c04"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Perempuan itu mengaku bahwa Fatlips adalah lelaki kecil yang merapikan ruangan ketika ia pergi dan menjaga sel tempatnya tinggal tetap kering dengan menginjak lembap dari tanah memakai sepatu besi yang berat. Ia keluar hanya pada malam hari dan tidak pernah meninggalkan ruang bawah tanah itu pada siang hari.",
   "en": "In her account, Fatlips was a small man who put the room in order during her absences and kept her cell dry by pounding the damp out of the ground in heavy iron boots. She came out only at night and never left the vault during the day.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "fat-lips-c03",
    "fat-lips-c04",
    "fat-lips-c05"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Teks kumpulan cerita rakyat kedua mencatat bahwa orang yang berpengetahuan memandang perempuan itu dengan iba sebagai orang yang terganggu akalnya, sedangkan orang awam memandangnya dengan rasa takut. Cara hidupnya diyakini berasal dari sumpah tidak akan memandang matahari selama kekasihnya pergi; kekasih itu gugur dalam perang saudara 1745–46. Ruang bawah tanah itu masih memakai nama Fatlips, dan hanya sedikit petani sekitar yang berani memasukinya pada malam hari.",
   "en": "The second source, a folk-tale collection, records that the well-informed regarded the woman with compassion as deranged in understanding, while the common people regarded her with terror. Her way of life was believed to come from a vow never to look upon the sun while her lover was away; the lover fell in the civil war of 1745-46. The vault still carries the name Fatlips, and few neighbouring peasants dare enter it by night.",
   "claim_ids": [

    "fat-lips-c06",
    "fat-lips-c07",
    "fat-lips-c08",
    "fat-lips-c09"
   ]
  }
 ],

 "claims": [
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c01",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s1",
   "quote": "Fat Lips (or Fatlips) is the name given to a legendary spirit dwelling in Dryburgh Abbey in Berwickshire, Scotland.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Fat Lips (Fatlips) adalah nama untuk roh legendaris yang menghuni Dryburgh Abbey di Berwickshire, Skotlandia.",
    "en": "Fat Lips (Fatlips) is the name given to a legendary spirit dwelling in Dryburgh Abbey in Berwickshire, Scotland."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c02",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s1",
   "quote": "The spirit was associated with a homeless woman who moved into a vault beneath the ruins of the abbey some time after the 1745 Jacobite rising.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Roh ini dikaitkan dengan seorang perempuan tunawisma yang pindah ke sebuah ruang bawah tanah di bawah reruntuhan biara beberapa waktu setelah pemberontakan Jacobite 1745.",
    "en": "The spirit was associated with a homeless woman who moved into a vault beneath the ruins of the abbey some time after the 1745 Jacobite rising."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c03",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s1",
   "quote": "was a little man who tidied the room while she was away, and kept the cell she lived in dry by stamping moisture away from the ground with his heavy iron boots.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Menurut perempuan itu, Fatlips adalah lelaki kecil yang merapikan ruangan saat ia pergi dan menjaga selnya tetap kering dengan menginjak-injak kelembapan dari tanah memakai sepatu bot besinya yang berat.",
    "en": "According to the woman, Fatlips was a little man who tidied the room while she was away and kept her cell dry by stamping moisture from the ground with his heavy iron boots."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c04",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s2",
   "quote": "an unfortunate female wanderer took up her residence in a dark vault, among the ruins of Dryburgh Abbey, which during the day, she never quitted.",
   "locator": "Fatlips, p. 203",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Seorang perempuan pengembara yang malang tinggal di sebuah ruang bawah tanah yang gelap di antara reruntuhan Dryburgh Abbey dan tidak pernah meninggalkannya pada siang hari.",
    "en": "An unfortunate female wanderer lived in a dark vault among the ruins of Dryburgh Abbey, which she never left during the day."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c05",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s2",
   "quote": "her habitation was arranged by a spirit, to whom she gave the uncouth name of Fatlips, describing him as a little man, wearing heavy iron shoes, with which he trampled the clay floor of the vault, to dispel the damps.",
   "locator": "Fatlips, p. 203",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Perempuan itu mengatakan tempat tinggalnya ditata oleh sebuah roh yang ia beri nama Fatlips; ia menggambarkannya sebagai lelaki kecil bersepatu besi berat yang menginjak lantai tanah liat ruang bawah tanah itu untuk mengusir lembap.",
    "en": "The woman said her dwelling was arranged by a spirit she named Fatlips, describing him as a little man in heavy iron shoes who trampled the clay floor of the vault to dispel the damps."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c06",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s2",
   "quote": "This circumstance caused her to be regarded, by the well-informed, with compassion, as deranged in her understanding; and by the vulgar, with some degree of terror.",
   "locator": "Fatlips, p. 203",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Karena hal ini, orang yang berpengetahuan memandangnya dengan iba sebagai orang yang terganggu akalnya, sedangkan orang awam memandangnya dengan rasa takut.",
    "en": "Because of this, the well-informed regarded her with compassion as deranged in understanding, and the common people regarded her with some terror."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c07",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s2",
   "quote": "It was, however, believed to have been occasioned by a vow that, during the absence of a man to whom she was attached, she would never look upon the sun. Her lover never returned.",
   "locator": "Fatlips, p. 203",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Cara hidupnya diyakini berasal dari sumpah bahwa selama lelaki yang dicintainya pergi, ia tidak akan memandang matahari. Kekasihnya tidak pernah kembali.",
    "en": "Her way of life was believed to stem from a vow that, while the man to whom she was attached was away, she would never look upon the sun. Her lover never returned."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c08",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s2",
   "quote": "He fell during the civil war of 1745-46, and she never more would behold the light of day.",
   "locator": "Fatlips, p. 203",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Kekasih itu gugur dalam perang saudara 1745–46, dan sejak itu ia tidak lagi melihat cahaya siang.",
    "en": "The lover fell during the civil war of 1745-46, and she never again beheld the light of day."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "fat-lips-c09",
   "source_id": "fat-lips-s2",
   "quote": "The vault, or rather dungeon, in which this unfortunate woman lived and died, passes still by the name of the supernatural being with which its gloom was tenanted by her disturbed imagination, and few of the neighbouring peasants dare enter it by night.",
   "locator": "Fatlips, p. 204",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Ruang bawah tanah tempat perempuan itu hidup dan meninggal masih dinamai menurut makhluk gaib yang menurut penulis dihuni oleh khayalannya yang terganggu, dan hanya sedikit petani sekitar yang berani memasukinya pada malam hari.",
    "en": "The vault where the woman lived and died still bears the name of the supernatural being with which, in the author's words, her disturbed imagination peopled its gloom, and few neighbouring peasants dare enter it by night."
   }
  }
 ],
```

```json
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Finfolk adalah ras penyihir dan pengubah wujud dari laut dalam cerita rakyat Orkney, yang menculik manusia dekat pantai untuk dijadikan pasangan.",
  "en": "The Finfolk are a race of sorcerous shapeshifters of the sea in Orkney folklore, who abduct humans near the shore to serve as spouses.",
  "claim_ids": [
   "finfolk-c01",
   "finfolk-c02"
  ]
 },
 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Orkney, Finfolk adalah pengubah wujud penyihir dari laut, ras gelap dan misterius yang berasal dari Finfolkaheem. Sumber kedua menyebut mereka khas bagi Orkney dan Shetland dan menggambarkan mereka sebagai bangsa laut amfibi yang bisa keluar dari air ke darat sesuka hati.",
   "en": "In Orkney folklore the Finfolk are sorcerous shapeshifters of the sea, a dark and mysterious race from Finfolkaheem. The second source calls them unique to Orkney and Shetland and describes an amphibious sea people who can step from the water onto land at will.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "finfolk-c01",
    "finfolk-c09",
    "finfolk-c10"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Finman digambarkan punya banyak kekuatan gaib, misalnya mendayung antara Norwegia dan Orkney dalam tujuh kayuhan, membuat kapalnya tak terlihat, dan menciptakan armada perahu hantu. Finwife memulai hidup sebagai putri duyung cantik yang bertekad mendapatkan suami manusia, dan anak perempuan Fin dikatakan lahir dengan ekor ikan yang terbelah menjadi kaki saat dewasa.",
   "en": "The Finman is credited with numerous magical abilities: crossing between Norway and Orkney in only seven strokes of the oar, hiding his boat from sight, and conjuring ghostly fleets. The Finwife begins life as a beautiful mermaid bent on acquiring a human husband, and daughters are said to be born with a fish tail that splits into legs at maturity.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "finfolk-c03",
    "finfolk-c05",
    "finfolk-c13"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Nelayan yang lengah dan anak muda yang bermain dekat pantai direnggut Finfolk dan dipaksa menikah seumur hidup. Mereka dikatakan memiliki dua tempat tinggal: Finfolkaheem di bawah laut tempat mereka tinggal pada musim dingin, dan pulau Hildaland.",
   "en": "Fishermen off guard and young people at play near the shore are snatched by the Finfolk and forced into a lifelong marriage. They were said to have two homes: Finfolkaheem under the sea, where they lived in winter, and the island of Hildaland.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "finfolk-c02",
    "finfolk-c06"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Kelemahan mereka antara lain perak dan tanda salib: menurut legenda koin perak yang dilempar menjauh dapat membantu orang lolos dari penculikan, dan Finman kadang dapat dihalau dengan salib yang digambar di dasar perahu memakai kapur atau ter.",
   "en": "Their weaknesses include silver and the cross: according to legend, silver coins tossed away can help a person escape abduction, and the Finman may sometimes be deterred by a cross drawn on the bottom of a boat with chalk or tar.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "finfolk-c04",
    "finfolk-c08"
   ]
  }
 ],

   "publisher": "Wikipedia",
   "published": null,
   "language": "en",
   "type": "wikipedia",
   "accessed": "2026-10-04"
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-s2",
   "url": "https://www.mysteriousbritain.co.uk/?p=407",
   "title": "Folklore And The Fin Folk of Orkney",
   "author": "Carolyn Emerick",
   "publisher": "Mysterious Britain & Ireland",
   "published": "2014",
   "language": "en",
   "type": "other",
   "accessed": "2026-10-04"
  }
 ],
 "claims": [
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c01",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "In Orkney folklore, Finfolk (sometimes Finnfolk) are sorcerous shapeshifters of the sea, the dark mysterious race from Finfolkaheem",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Orkney, Finfolk (kadang Finnfolk) adalah pengubah wujud penyihir dari laut, ras gelap dan misterius dari Finfolkaheem.",
    "en": "In Orkney folklore, Finfolk (sometimes Finnfolk) are sorcerous shapeshifters of the sea, the dark mysterious race from Finfolkaheem."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c02",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "The Finfolk (both Finman and Finwife) kidnap unsuspecting fishermen, or frolicking youth, near the shore and force them into lifelong servitude as a spouse.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Finfolk (baik Finman maupun Finwife) menculik nelayan yang lengah atau pemuda yang bermain dekat pantai dan memaksa mereka menjadi pasangan seumur hidup.",
    "en": "The Finfolk (both Finman and Finwife) kidnap unsuspecting fishermen or frolicking youth near the shore and force them into lifelong servitude as a spouse."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c03",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "He is said to have many magical powers, such as rowing between Norway and Orkney in seven oar-strokes, making his ship invisible and creating fleets of phantom boats.",
   "locator": "The Finfolk – Finman",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Finman dikatakan punya banyak kekuatan gaib, seperti mendayung antara Norwegia dan Orkney dalam tujuh kayuhan, membuat kapalnya tak terlihat, dan menciptakan armada perahu hantu.",
    "en": "The Finman is said to have many magical powers, such as rowing between Norway and Orkney in seven oar-strokes, making his ship invisible and creating fleets of phantom boats."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c04",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "though he may sometimes be deterred by drawing a cross on the bottom of a craft with chalk or tar, for Finfolk abhor the sign of the Christian cross",
   "locator": "The Finfolk – Finman",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Finman kadang dapat dihalau dengan menggambar salib di dasar perahu memakai kapur atau ter, karena Finfolk membenci tanda salib Kristen.",
    "en": "The Finman may sometimes be deterred by drawing a cross on the bottom of a craft with chalk or tar, for the Finfolk abhor the sign of the Christian cross."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c05",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "The Finwife starts her life as a beautiful mermaid bent on acquiring a human husband.",
   "locator": "The Finfolk – Finwife",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Finwife memulai hidupnya sebagai putri duyung cantik yang bertekad mendapatkan suami manusia.",
    "en": "The Finwife starts her life as a beautiful mermaid bent on acquiring a human husband."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c06",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "The Finfolk were said to have two homes: the magical underwater world of Finfolkaheem where they lived in the winter, and the island of Hildaland.",
   "locator": "Homes",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Finfolk dikatakan punya dua tempat tinggal: dunia bawah laut ajaib Finfolkaheem tempat mereka tinggal pada musim dingin, dan pulau Hildaland.",
    "en": "The Finfolk were said to have two homes: the magical underwater world of Finfolkaheem where they lived in the winter, and the island of Hildaland."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c07",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "a paradisiacal island that was said to either be invisible, hidden just underwater, or surrounded by magical fog.",
   "locator": "Homes – Hildaland",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Hildaland digambarkan sebagai pulau surgawi yang dikatakan tak terlihat, tersembunyi tepat di bawah air, atau diselimuti kabut gaib.",
    "en": "Hildaland is described as a paradisiacal island said to be invisible, hidden just underwater, or surrounded by magical fog."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c08",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s1",
   "quote": "Finfolk, in addition to their lust for humans, have a weakness for silver, including silver coins and jewelry. According to legend, a possible way to escape abduction is to exploit this Finfolk weakness by tossing silver coins away from oneself.",
   "locator": "Human abduction",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Selain nafsunya pada manusia, Finfolk lemah terhadap perak; menurut legenda, cara lolos dari penculikan ialah melempar koin perak menjauh dari diri.",
    "en": "Besides their lust for humans, Finfolk have a weakness for silver; according to legend, a way to escape abduction is to toss silver coins away from oneself."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c09",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s2",
   "quote": "One mythological race of beings which embodies all of the influences discussed above is the Fin Folk, who are unique to Orkney and Shetland.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Fin Folk disebut sebagai ras mitologis yang khas bagi Orkney dan Shetland.",
    "en": "The Fin Folk are described as a mythological race unique to Orkney and Shetland."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c10",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s2",
   "quote": "their race is that of an amphibious sea people who can step out of the water onto land as they choose, and live comfortably between the two worlds. They are both threatening and benevolent.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Fin Folk adalah bangsa laut amfibi yang dapat keluar dari air ke darat sesuka hati, dan digambarkan sekaligus mengancam dan baik hati.",
    "en": "The Fin Folk are an amphibious sea people who can step from the water onto land at will, and are described as both threatening and benevolent."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c11",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s2",
   "quote": "Known as powerful sorcerers, Fin Folk can control the weather. They may reward fishermen with pleasant sea waters, or punish them with treacherous storms.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dikenal sebagai penyihir ampuh, Fin Folk dapat mengendalikan cuaca; mereka bisa memberi nelayan laut yang tenang atau menghukum dengan badai berbahaya.",
    "en": "Known as powerful sorcerers, Fin Folk can control the weather; they may reward fishermen with pleasant seas or punish them with treacherous storms."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c12",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s2",
   "quote": "But never silver, for Fin Folk value silver greatly and are loathe parting with it.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Fin Folk sangat menghargai perak dan enggan melepaskannya.",
    "en": "Fin Folk value silver greatly and are loath to part with it."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "finfolk-c13",
   "source_id": "finfolk-s2",
   "quote": "Female Fin children, however, are born with the fish tail of a mermaid, which they wear until they reach maturity when their tail will split into legs.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Anak perempuan Fin lahir dengan ekor ikan putri duyung, yang mereka pakai sampai dewasa ketika ekor itu terbelah menjadi kaki.",
    "en": "Female Fin children are born with the fish tail of a mermaid, which they wear until maturity when the tail splits into legs."
   }
  }
 ],
 "conflicts": [
  {
   "topic": {
    "id": "Sifat Fin Folk terhadap manusia",
    "en": "The Fin Folk's disposition toward humans"
   },
   "positions": [
    {
     "summary": {
      "id": "Artikel pertama menggambarkan mereka serakah dan menculik manusia.",
      "en": "The first article portrays them as abductors of humans."
     },
     "claim_ids": [
      "finfolk-c02"
     ]
    },
    {
     "summary": {
      "id": "Artikel kedua menyebut mereka sekaligus mengancam dan baik hati, dan dapat memberi atau menghukum nelayan lewat cuaca.",
      "en": "The second article calls them both threatening and benevolent, able to reward or punish fishermen through the weather."
     },
     "claim_ids": [

    }
   ]

 ],
 "gaps": [
  {
   "field": "etymology",
   "searched": "Artikel Wikipedia memberi arti harfiah nama Finfolkaheem dengan tanda kutip; tidak dipakai agar kutipan tetap persis."
  },
  {

  },
  {

  },

 ]
```

```json
 "disposition": null,
 "traits": [],
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Freybug disebut sebagai Anjing Hitam mengerikan dari cerita rakyat Inggris abad pertengahan, khususnya Norfolk, yang menakuti pelancong di jalan desa.",
  "en": "The Freybug is described as a monstrous Black Dog of medieval English folklore, specifically Norfolk, that terrified travelers on country roads.",
  "claim_ids": [
   "freybug-c01",
   "freybug-c02"
  ]
 },
 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Freybug dinyatakan sebagai Anjing Hitam mengerikan yang berasal dari cerita rakyat Inggris abad pertengahan, khususnya dari Norfolk. Seperti kebanyakan anjing hitam gaib, ukurannya kira-kira sebesar anak sapi dan berkeliaran di jalan pedesaan untuk menakuti para pelancong.",
   "en": "The first source describes the Freybug as a fearsome Black Dog out of medieval English folklore, tied in particular to Norfolk. As is typical of uncanny black dogs, it stood about as tall as a calf and prowled rural lanes, frightening those who travelled them.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "freybug-c01",
    "freybug-c02"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Laurence Saunders, martir Inggris, menyebut Fray-bug dalam surat-suratnya kepada istrinya pada 1555. Kamus sejarah mendefinisikan fray-bug sebagai objek yang ditakuti, yaitu hantu atau momok, dan mengutip kalimat Saunders yang memuat kata itu.",
   "en": "Laurence Saunders, an English martyr, mentioned Fray-bugs in his letters to his wife in 1555. A historical dictionary defines a fray-bug as an object of fear, a bogy or spectre, and cites the Saunders sentence that uses the word.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "freybug-c03",
    "freybug-c05",
    "freybug-c06"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Gagasan bahwa Fray-bug adalah Anjing Hitam mirip Barghest berasal dari tafsiran John Brand, bukan dari definisi kamus itu sendiri.",
   "en": "The idea that the Fray-bug was a Black Dog like the Barghest comes from John Brand's interpretation, not from the dictionary definition itself.",

   ]
```

```json
  "id": "Grant adalah makhluk dalam cerita rakyat Inggris yang digambarkan seperti anak kuda yang berdiri di atas kaki belakang dan berlari di jalan-jalan sebagai peringatan bahaya api.",
  "en": "The Grant is a creature of English folklore described as a foal standing on its hind legs that runs through the streets as a warning of fire.",
  "claim_ids": [
   "grant-folklore-c01",
   "grant-folklore-c06",
   "grant-folklore-c08"
  ]
 },
 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Grant, atau Gyant, adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Inggris yang dijelaskan dalam karya Latin abad pertengahan Otia Imperialia oleh Gervase dari Tilbury. Menurut kutipan dari Gervase, ia sejenis iblis yang berwujud seperti anak kuda berumur setahun, berdiri tegak pada kaki belakang, dengan mata berkilau.",
   "en": "The Grant (also spelled Gyant) is an English folklore creature known from the medieval Latin Otia Imperialia of Gervase of Tilbury. According to the passage quoted from Gervase, it is a kind of demon that looks like a yearling foal, upright on its hind legs, with sparkling eyes.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "grant-folklore-c01",
    "grant-folklore-c05",
    "grant-folklore-c06"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Bila bahaya mengancam pada hari atau malam berikutnya, Grant berlari di jalan-jalan dan memancing anjing menggonggong. Peristiwa ini memperingatkan penduduk agar waspada terhadap api, sehingga Gervase menyebutnya iblis yang bersahabat sekalipun ia menakuti orang yang melihatnya.",
   "en": "When danger is impending the following day or night, the Grant runs about the streets and provokes the dogs to bark. This warns the inhabitants to beware of fire, so Gervase calls it a friendly demon even though it frightens those who see it.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "grant-folklore-c07",
    "grant-folklore-c08"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Catatan Gervase adalah satu-satunya sumber abad pertengahan yang diketahui yang menyebut nama makhluk ini. Francis Young mengaitkannya dengan motif momok mirip kuda di jalan terpencil di Britania dan Irlandia, sedangkan Joseph Pentangelo berpendapat bahwa ia mungkin seekor kelinci liar dan gambaran Gervase dipoles oleh Gervase atau narasumbernya.",
   "en": "No other medieval source is known to name the creature besides Gervase. Francis Young links it to the motif of horse-like bogies on isolated roads in Britain and Ireland, while Joseph Pentangelo argues that it was probably a hare and that Gervase's description was embellished by Gervase or his informant.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "grant-folklore-c02",
    "grant-folklore-c03",
    "grant-folklore-c04"
   ]
  }
 ],

   "source_id": "grant-folklore-s2",

   "source_id": "grant-folklore-s2",
```

```json
 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Gwedros Gawr (juga Gwedraws atau Gwaedros) adalah raksasa yang menurut legenda tinggal di Caerwedros di Ceredigion. Namanya disebut dalam buku sang antikuaris abad ke-17 Siôn Dafydd Rhys, yang memuat lebih dari lima puluh nama raksasa Wales dan kini menjadi bagian dari Naskah Peniarth di Perpustakaan Nasional Wales.",
   "en": "Gwedros Gawr (also Gwedraws or Gwaedros) is a giant who, according to legend, lived at Caerwedros in Ceredigion. His name appears in the book of the 17th-century antiquarian Siôn Dafydd Rhys, which lists over fifty names of giants of Wales and is now held among the Peniarth Manuscripts in the National Library of Wales.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "gwedros-gawr-c01",
    "gwedros-gawr-c02",
    "gwedros-gawr-c03"

  {
   "id": "Teks Rhys menempatkan sebuah tempat bernama Caer Wedros di paroki Llan Dyssiliaw, yang dinamai karena Gwedros Gawr dahulu tinggal di sana. Caerwedros kini adalah desa kecil di Ceredigion barat; situs Kastil Caerwedros berupa gundukan tanah di sisi barat desa.",
   "en": "Rhys's text locates a place called Caer Wedros in the parish of Llan Dyssiliaw, named because Gwedros Gawr formerly dwelt there. Caerwedros is now a small village in western Ceredigion; the site of Caerwedros Castle is a mound of earth on the west side of the village.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "gwedros-gawr-c04",
```

```json
 "traits": [],
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Gwyllion adalah peri perempuan berwujud menakutkan dalam cerita rakyat Wales yang menghantui jalan sepi di pegunungan dan menyesatkan pengelana.",
  "en": "The Gwyllion are female fairies of frightful aspect in Welsh folklore who haunt lonely mountain roads and lead travellers astray.",
  "claim_ids": [
   "gwyllion-c01",
   "gwyllion-c07"
  ]
 },
 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Menurut Wirt Sikes, gwyllion adalah peri perempuan berwujud menakutkan yang menghantui jalan sepi di pegunungan Wales dan menyesatkan pengelana. Mereka dilukiskan sebagai roh muram yang lebih mirip hag atau penyihir, berbeda dari ellyllon yang lebih baik hati. Siapa pun yang bertemu mereka, pada malam hari atau hari berkabut, pasti tersesat.",
   "en": "Wirt Sikes describes the gwyllion as frightening female fairies that haunt isolated Welsh mountain roads and mislead wayfarers. They are portrayed as gloomy spirits more akin to hags or witches than the more benevolent ellyllon. Anyone who meets them, by night or on a misty day, is sure to lose the way.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "gwyllion-c01",
    "gwyllion-c02",
    "gwyllion-c04",
    "gwyllion-c07",
    "gwyllion-c09"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Satu gwyll yang paling dikenal ialah Old Woman of the Mountain, yang menghantui Gunung Llanhyddel di Monmouthshire dan dianggap mewakili seluruh jenisnya. Tradisi setempat menyebutnya arwah seorang Juan White yang dianggap penyihir.",
   "en": "The best-known gwyll is the Old Woman of the Mountain, said to have haunted the Llanhyddel Mountain of Monmouthshire and said to typify all her kind. Local tradition held her to be the spirit of a Juan White who was thought to be a witch.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "gwyllion-c03",
    "gwyllion-c10",
    "gwyllion-c11"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Menurut Jones, di Aberystruth Gwyllion sering masuk ke rumah penduduk saat badai. Mereka disambut dengan air bersih dan penghuni memastikan tidak ada pisau di sudut dekat api, karena orang takut dicelakai bila mereka tersinggung. Di luar rumah, pisau yang dihunus dipercaya dapat mengusir mereka.",
   "en": "According to Jones, at Aberystruth the Gwyllion often entered people's houses in stormy weather. They were welcomed with clean water and no knife was left in the corner near the fire, because people feared harm if the fairies were offended. Outdoors, a drawn knife was believed to drive them off.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "gwyllion-c05",
    "gwyllion-c12",
    "gwyllion-c13",
    "gwyllion-c14"
   ]
  }
 ],

   "source_id": "gwyllion-s2",
   "quote": "The Welsh word gwyll is variously used to signify gloom, shade, duskiness, a hag, a witch, a fairy, and a goblin",
   "locator": "Bab Mountain Fairies—The Gwyllion",
   "context": "etymology",
   "statement": {

    "en": "The Welsh word gwyll is variously used to signify gloom, shade, duskiness, a hag, a witch, a fairy and a goblin."

   "id": "gwyllion-c09",
   "source_id": "gwyllion-s2",
   "quote": "but its special application is to these mountain fairies of gloomy and harmful habits, as distinct from the Ellyllon of the forest glades and dingles, which are more often beneficent.",
   "locator": "Bab Mountain Fairies—The Gwyllion",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Pemakaian khususnya ialah untuk peri-peri gunung yang berkebiasaan muram dan merugikan, berbeda dari Ellyllon di padang hutan dan lembah, yang lebih sering baik hati.",
    "en": "Its special application is to these mountain fairies of gloomy and harmful habits, as distinct from the Ellyllon of the forest glades and dingles, which are more often beneficent."

  },

   "id": "gwyllion-c10",
   "source_id": "gwyllion-s2",
   "quote": "and the Old Woman of the Mountain typifies all her kind.",
   "locator": "Bab Mountain Fairies—The Gwyllion",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {

    "en": "The Old Woman of the Mountain typifies all her kind."

  },
  {
   "id": "gwyllion-c11",
   "source_id": "gwyllion-s2",
   "quote": "The popular tradition in that district was that the Old Woman of the Mountain was the spirit of one Juan White, who lived time out of mind in those parts, and was thought to be a witch",
   "locator": "Bab Mountain Fairies—The Gwyllion",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Tradisi populer di daerah itu menyebut Old Woman of the Mountain adalah arwah seorang Juan White, yang hidup sejak dahulu kala di sana dan dianggap penyihir.",
    "en": "The popular tradition in that district was that the Old Woman of the Mountain was the spirit of one Juan White, who lived time out of mind in those parts and was thought to be a witch."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "gwyllion-c12",
   "source_id": "gwyllion-s2",
   "quote": "In Wales, according to Jones, the Gwyllion often came into the houses of the people at Aberystruth, especially in stormy weather",
   "locator": "Bab Mountain Fairies—The Gwyllion",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {

    "en": "In Wales, according to Jones, the Gwyllion often came into the houses of the people at Aberystruth, especially in stormy weather."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "gwyllion-c13",
   "source_id": "gwyllion-s2",
   "quote": "providing clean water for them, and taking especial care that no knife, or other cutting tool, should be in the corner near the fire, where the fairies would go to sit.",
   "locator": "Bab Mountain Fairies—The Gwyllion",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Penghuni menyambut mereka dengan menyediakan air bersih dan memastikan tidak ada pisau atau alat pemotong di sudut dekat api tempat para peri duduk.",
    "en": "Inmates welcomed them by providing clean water and taking care that no knife or cutting tool lay in the corner near the fire where the fairies sat."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "gwyllion-c14",
   "source_id": "gwyllion-s2",
   "quote": "So he drew out his knife, and the fairies vanished directly.",

   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Maka ia menghunus pisaunya, dan para peri langsung lenyap.",
    "en": "So he drew out his knife, and the fairies vanished directly."

  },
  {
   "id": "gwyllion-c15",
   "source_id": "gwyllion-s1",
   "quote": "He tried to overtake her but she led him further astray, always out of reach, until he found himself in a marsh.",
   "locator": "Folklore",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
```

```json
 "traits": [],
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Hantu Hampton Court adalah sosok berjubah panjang yang terekam kamera CCTV di Istana Hampton Court pada Oktober 2003 ketika sebuah pintu darurat berulang kali terbuka sendiri.",
  "en": "The Hampton Court ghost is a figure in a long robe captured by CCTV at Hampton Court Palace in October 2003, when a fire door repeatedly opened by itself.",
  "claim_ids": [
   "hampton-court-ghost-c01",
   "hampton-court-ghost-c02",
   "hampton-court-ghost-c04"
  ]
 },
 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Sebutan hantu Hampton Court merujuk pada sosok yang tampak dalam rekaman CCTV di dekat Istana Hampton Court pada Oktober 2003. Sosok itu mengenakan kain panjang mirip jubah dan diduga menghantui tempat itu selama beberapa hari atau bulan. Istana abad ke-16 di Surrey itu memang sudah lama punya reputasi berhantu.",
   "en": "The name Hampton Court ghost refers to a figure seen on CCTV footage near Hampton Court Palace in October 2003. The figure wore a long robe-like cloth and was said to haunt the place for some days or months. The 16th-century palace in Surrey already had a long reputation for being haunted.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "hampton-court-ghost-c01",
    "hampton-court-ghost-c02",
    "hampton-court-ghost-c07"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Petugas keamanan berulang kali mendapati pintu darurat di sebuah area pameran terbuka. Pada kesempatan pertama rekaman hanya menunjukkan pintu terbanting terbuka tanpa sebab yang terlihat; pada kesempatan kedua muncul sosok berpakaian zaman dahulu yang lalu menutup pintu itu; pada hari ketiga pintu kembali terbuka tetapi tidak ada sosok yang tampak. Bahkan sebelumnya seorang pengunjung menulis di buku tamu istana bahwa ia merasa melihat hantu di area yang sama.",
   "en": "Security staff repeatedly found a fire door standing open in an exhibition area. The first time, the footage showed only the doors flying open with no visible cause; the second time, a figure in period dress appeared and closed them; on a third day the same happened but no figure was seen. Earlier still, a visitor had noted in the palace's visitor book a belief of having encountered a ghost in that same area.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "hampton-court-ghost-c04",
    "hampton-court-ghost-c08",
    "hampton-court-ghost-c09"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Dalam foto diam yang dirilis istana, sosok pria berpakaian mirip jubah melangkah keluar dari ambang pintu yang gelap sambil menjulurkan satu lengan ke gagang pintu. Wajahnya tampak putih tidak wajar dibandingkan tangannya, dan seorang penjaga, James Faukes, berkata wajah itu tidak tampak seperti wajah manusia. Penjaga lain, Ian Franklin, menggambarkan sosok bermantel penuh yang menarik kedua daun pintu lalu menutupnya di belakangnya.",
   "en": "In the still photograph released by the palace, a man in a robe-like garment steps out of the shadowy doorway, one arm reaching for the door handle. His face looks unnaturally white beside his hand, and a warder, James Faukes, said the face did not look human. Another warder, Ian Franklin, described a figure in a full cloak pulling in both doors and closing them behind it.",
   "claim_ids": [

   "en": "According to the first article, the figure was first reported in early 2001, and a similar incident in 2015 was later debunked.  "claims": [
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c01",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s1",
   "quote": "Hampton Court ghost refers to a figure seen in a CCTV footage/image near Hampton Court Palace in October 2003.",
   "locator": "Lead",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Hantu Hampton Court merujuk pada sosok yang terlihat dalam rekaman CCTV di dekat Istana Hampton Court pada Oktober 2003.",
    "en": "The Hampton Court ghost refers to a figure seen in CCTV footage near Hampton Court Palace in October 2003."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c02",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s1",
   "quote": "The figure was seen in a long robe-like cloth allegedly haunting the place for a couple of days or months.",
   "locator": "Lead",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Sosok itu terlihat mengenakan kain panjang mirip jubah dan diduga menghantui tempat itu selama beberapa hari atau bulan.",
    "en": "The figure was seen in a long robe-like cloth, allegedly haunting the place for a couple of days or months."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c03",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s1",
   "quote": "The figure was first reported in early-2001. A similar incident happened in 2015 but was later debunked.",
   "locator": "Lead",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Sosok itu pertama kali dilaporkan pada awal 2001; kejadian serupa pada 2015 kemudian dibantah.",
    "en": "The figure was first reported in early 2001; a similar incident in 2015 was later debunked."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c04",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s2",
   "quote": "Security guards spotted that fire doors kept opening in an exhibition area, and on one occasion checked the footage and saw a figure in period dress.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Petugas keamanan mendapati pintu darurat di area pameran terus terbuka, lalu memeriksa rekaman dan melihat sosok berpakaian zaman dahulu.",
    "en": "Security guards noticed fire doors kept opening in an exhibition area, checked the footage and saw a figure in period dress."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c05",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s2",
   "quote": "It was incredibly spooky because the face just didn't look human,\" he said.",
   "locator": "Article body (warder James Faukes)",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Penjaga James Faukes berkata wajah sosok itu tidak tampak seperti wajah manusia.",
    "en": "Warder James Faukes said it was spooky because the face just didn't look human."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c06",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s2",
   "quote": "State apartment warder Ian Franklin added: \"Someone who appears to be in a full cloak walks forward, brings one door in, another door in and then closes it behind them.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Penjaga Ian Franklin menggambarkan sosok bermantel penuh yang menarik kedua daun pintu lalu menutupnya di belakangnya.",
    "en": "Warder Ian Franklin described someone in a full cloak walking forward, bringing in both doors and closing them behind them."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c07",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s2",
   "quote": "CCTV cameras picked up the vision at the 16th Century Surrey palace, which has a reputation for being haunted.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Kamera CCTV menangkap penampakan itu di istana abad ke-16 di Surrey yang punya reputasi berhantu.",
    "en": "CCTV cameras picked up the vision at the 16th-century Surrey palace, which has a reputation for being haunted."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c08",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s3",
   "quote": "On the first occasion the footage showed the doors flying wide open, but no evidence of why they had. On the second, the guards were stunned when a ghostly figure in period dress suddenly appeared on the screen and closed the doors.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Pertama kali rekaman menunjukkan pintu terbuka lebar tanpa sebab; kedua kalinya sosok seperti hantu berpakaian zaman dahulu muncul dan menutup pintu.",
    "en": "The first footage showed the doors flying open with no evidence why; the second time a ghostly figure in period dress appeared on screen and closed the doors."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c09",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s3",
   "quote": "The same thing happened on a third day, but again no figure appeared. Stranger still was that a visitor had noted in the palace's visitor book that she thought she had seen a ghost in that area.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Hal serupa terjadi pada hari ketiga tanpa sosok yang tampak; seorang pengunjung juga menulis di buku tamu istana bahwa ia merasa melihat hantu di area itu.",
    "en": "The same happened on a third day with no figure appearing; a visitor had also noted in the palace visitor book that she thought she had seen a ghost in that area."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c10",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s4",
   "quote": "The area around the man is somewhat blurred, and his face appears unnaturally white compared with his outstretched hand.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Area di sekitar pria itu agak buram dan wajahnya tampak putih tidak wajar dibandingkan tangannya yang terjulur.",
    "en": "The area around the man is somewhat blurred, and his face appears unnaturally white compared with his outstretched hand."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c11",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s4",
   "quote": "In the still photograph, the figure of a man in a robelike garment is shown stepping from the shadowy doorway, one arm reaching out for the door handle.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dalam foto diam, sosok pria berpakaian mirip jubah tampak melangkah dari ambang pintu yang gelap sambil meraih gagang pintu.",
    "en": "In the still photograph, a man in a robelike garment is shown stepping from the shadowy doorway, one arm reaching for the door handle."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c12",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s5",
   "quote": "A dark, distorted figure popped up in a picture recently taken by a 12-year-old girl while she and a cousin were visiting the tourist-friendly palace.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "modern-popular-culture",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Sosok gelap yang terdistorsi muncul dalam foto yang diambil seorang anak perempuan berumur 12 tahun saat mengunjungi istana.",
    "en": "A dark, distorted figure appeared in a picture taken by a 12-year-old girl while visiting the palace."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c13",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s5",
   "quote": "Metabunk.org's Mick West says it looks like the weird effect that's created when someone walks through your line of sight while you're taking an iPhone panorama.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Mick West menilai foto 2015 itu mirip efek yang muncul ketika seseorang lewat di depan kamera saat mengambil panorama iPhone.",
    "en": "Mick West says the 2015 photo looks like the effect created when someone walks through your line of sight while taking an iPhone panorama."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c14",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s2",
   "quote": "I thought someone was having a laugh but our costumed guides don't own a costume like that.",
   "locator": "Article body (warder James Faukes)",
   "context": "historical-record",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Penjaga itu semula mengira ada yang bercanda, tetapi pemandu berkostum di istana tidak memiliki kostum seperti itu.",
    "en": "The warder first thought someone was having a laugh, but the costumed guides don't own a costume like that."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "hampton-court-ghost-c15",
   "source_id": "hampton-court-ghost-s5",
   "quote": "This time, it's about a picture purporting to show the \"Grey Lady,\" the shade of a servant who is said to have nursed Queen Elizabeth I and died of smallpox in 1562.",
   "locator": "Article body",
   "context": "modern-popular-culture",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Foto tahun 2015 itu diklaim menunjukkan Grey Lady, arwah seorang pelayan yang konon mengasuh Ratu Elizabeth I dan meninggal karena cacar pada 1562.",
    "en": "The 2015 picture purported to show the Grey Lady, the shade of a servant said to have nursed Queen Elizabeth I and died of smallpox in 1562."
   }
  }
 ],
```

```json
 "traits": [],
 "short_description": {
  "id": "Iannic-ann-ôd (Yannig an Aod, \"Jean Kecil dari pantai\") adalah jiwa orang tenggelam yang jasadnya tidak ditemukan, yang menurut cerita rakyat Breton terdengar menjerit di pantai pada malam hari.",
  "en": "Iannic-ann-ôd (Yannig an Aod, \"Little John of the shore\") is, in Breton folklore, the lost soul of a drowned person whose body was never recovered, heard crying along the coast at night.",
  "claim_ids": [
   "iannic-ann-od-c01",
   "iannic-ann-od-c02",
   "iannic-ann-od-c03",
   "iannic-ann-od-c05"
  ]
 },
 "long_description": [
  {
   "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Breton, Iannic-ann-ôd atau Yannig an Aod dikatakan sebagai jiwa-jiwa orang yang tenggelam di laut dan tidak pernah ditemukan jasadnya. Teks kedua menyebut mereka mengembara selamanya di sepanjang pantai karena jasadnya tidak dikuburkan di tanah suci, dan nama Iannic-ann-ôd dipakai untuk semua orang tenggelam yang melolong itu.",
   "en": "In Breton folklore, Iannic-ann-ôd or Yannig an Aod are said to be the souls of people drowned at sea whose bodies were never recovered. The second source says they wander forever along the coasts because their bodies were not buried in sacred ground, and the name Iannic-ann-ôd is used for all of these howling drowned.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "iannic-ann-od-c01",
    "iannic-ann-od-c02",
    "iannic-ann-od-c03",
    "iannic-ann-od-c07"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Mereka terdengar menjerit pilu pada malam hari dengan seruan \"Iou! Iou!\". Di Cornouailles orang berkata bahwa Iannic-ann-ôd, yang berarti \"Petit-Jean de la grève\" atau Jean Kecil dari pantai, sedang melolong.",
   "en": "They are heard crying dismally at night with the call \"Iou! Iou!\". In Cornouailles people say that Iannic-ann-ôd, \"Petit-Jean de la grève\" or Little John of the shore, is howling.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "iannic-ann-od-c02",
    "iannic-ann-od-c04",
    "iannic-ann-od-c05",
    "iannic-ann-od-c06"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Menurut teks kedua, Iannic-ann-ôd tidak jahat selama orang tidak membalas ratapannya. Bila dijawab sekali ia melompat setengah jarak, bila dijawab dua kali ia melompat setengah dari sisanya, dan bila dijawab tiga kali ia mematahkan leher orang itu.",
   "en": "According to the second text, Iannic-ann-ôd is not wicked as long as no one answers his lament. If answered once he leaps half the distance, if answered twice he leaps half of what remains, and if answered three times he breaks the person's neck.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "iannic-ann-od-c08",
    "iannic-ann-od-c09"
   ]
  },
  {
   "id": "Teks yang sama juga memuat sebuah cerita tentang pelayan pertanian yang sengaja menjawab seruan itu: ia menunggu sampai dekat pertanian, menjawab ketiga kalinya sambil menutup pintu, dan pintu itu dihantam dengan keras dari luar. Suara di luar menyatakan akan memaafkan sekali ini saja.",
   "en": "The same text also contains a tale of a farm servant who deliberately answered the cry: he waited until near the farm, gave the third answer while closing the door, and the door was struck violently from outside. The voice outside declared it would forgive him this once.",
   "claim_ids": [
    "iannic-ann-od-c10",
    "iannic-ann-od-c11",
    "iannic-ann-od-c12",
    "iannic-ann-od-c13"
   ]
  }
 ],

 "claims": [
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c01",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s1",
   "quote": "In Breton folklore, Iannic-ann-ôd or Yannig an Aod",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Breton, makhluk ini disebut Iannic-ann-ôd atau Yannig an Aod.",
    "en": "In Breton folklore the being is called Iannic-ann-ôd or Yannig an Aod."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c02",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s1",
   "quote": "are said to be the lost souls of those drowned at sea and were never recovered.",
   "locator": "Intro",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Iannic-ann-ôd dikatakan sebagai jiwa-jiwa tersesat orang-orang yang tenggelam di laut dan jasadnya tidak pernah ditemukan.",
    "en": "Iannic-ann-ôd are said to be the lost souls of those drowned at sea who were never recovered."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c03",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "Les noyés, dont le corps n’a pas été retrouvé et enseveli en terre sacrée, errent éternellement le long des côtes.",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Orang tenggelam (les noyés) yang jasadnya tidak ditemukan dan dikubur di tanah suci mengembara selamanya menyusuri pantai.",
    "en": "The drowned (les noyés) whose bodies were not found and buried in sacred ground wander eternally along the coasts."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c04",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "Il n’est pas rare qu’on les entende crier, dans la nuit, lugubrement :",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Tidak jarang mereka terdengar menjerit pilu pada malam hari (crier, dans la nuit, lugubrement), dengan seruan \"Iou! Iou!\".",
    "en": "It is not rare to hear them cry out dismally in the night (crier, dans la nuit, lugubrement), with the call \"Iou! Iou!\"."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c05",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "E-man-Iannic-ann-ôd o iouall ! (Voilà Iannic-ann-ôd, — Petit-Jean de la grève, — qui hurle !)",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "etymology",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Di pelosok Cornouailles orang berseru bahwa Iannic-ann-ôd, \"Petit-Jean de la grève\" (Jean Kecil dari pantai), sedang melolong.",
    "en": "The cry goes that Iannic-ann-ôd, \"Petit-Jean de la grève\" (Little John of the shore), is howling."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c06",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "On dit alors, dans le pays de Cornouailles :",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Ungkapan itu diucapkan di wilayah Cornouailles (pays de Cornouailles).",
    "en": "The phrase is spoken in the country of Cornouailles (pays de Cornouailles)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c07",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "Tous ces noyés hurleurs sont instinctivement appelés Iannic-ann-ôd.",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Semua orang tenggelam yang melolong itu secara naluriah disebut Iannic-ann-ôd.",
    "en": "All these howling drowned are instinctively called Iannic-ann-ôd."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c08",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "Iannic-ann-ôd n’est pas méchant, pourvu qu’on ne s’amuse pas à lui renvoyer sa plainte sinistre.",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Iannic-ann-ôd tidak jahat (n'est pas méchant), asalkan orang tidak iseng membalas ratapannya yang suram.",
    "en": "Iannic-ann-ôd is not wicked (n'est pas méchant), provided one does not amuse oneself by sending back his sinister lament."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c09",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "si vous répondez une première fois, Iannic-ann-ôd franchit d’un bon la moitié de la distance qui le sépare de vous ; si vous répondez une deuxième fois, il franchit la moitié de cette moitié ; si vous répondez une troisième fois, il vous rompt le cou.",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Jika dijawab sekali, ia melompat setengah jarak ke arah orang itu; jika dijawab kedua kali, setengah dari sisa jarak; jika dijawab ketiga kali, ia mematahkan leher orang itu (vous rompt le cou).",
    "en": "If answered once he leaps half the distance toward you; a second time, half of the remainder; a third time, he breaks your neck (vous rompt le cou)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c10",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "Un domestique de ferme revenait de conduire les bêtes aux champs, un soir d’été, dans le temps où l’on commence à leur faire passer les nuits dehors.",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Dalam satu cerita, seorang pelayan pertanian (domestique de ferme) pulang dari menggiring ternak ke ladang pada suatu malam musim panas.",
    "en": "In one tale a farm servant (domestique de ferme) was coming back from driving the animals to the fields one summer evening."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c11",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "il attendit d’être assez près de la ferme, avant de répondre aux « Iou » stridents, que poussait derrière lui le rôdeur de plages.",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Pelayan itu menunggu sampai cukup dekat dengan pertanian sebelum menjawab seruan \"Iou\" yang nyaring dari sang pengembara pantai (rôdeur de plages) di belakangnya.",
    "en": "The servant waited until he was close to the farm before answering the shrill \"Iou\" calls of the beach prowler (rôdeur de plages) behind him."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c12",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "Un formidable coup s’abattit du dehors sur la porte ; on eût juré qu’elle volait en éclats.",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Setelah jawaban ketiga, sebuah pukulan dahsyat menghantam pintu dari luar hingga seakan pecah berkeping-keping (volait en éclats).",
    "en": "After the third answer a tremendous blow struck the door from outside, as if it were flying to pieces (volait en éclats)."
   }
  },
  {
   "id": "iannic-ann-od-c13",
   "source_id": "iannic-ann-od-s2",
   "quote": "Passe pour une fois : mais si tu y reviens, je ferai de toi un homme !",
   "locator": "Bab XLVII",
   "context": "traditional-belief",
   "statement": {
    "id": "Suara di luar mengancam: kali ini dimaafkan, tetapi jika pelayan itu mengulanginya, ia akan dijadikan \"un homme\" (manusia, dalam nada ancaman).",
    "en": "The voice outside threatens: it will pass this once, but if the servant tries it again he will be made \"un homme\"."
   }
  }
 ],
```
